# Task 02 — The unchanged skill and agent are measured on the eight cells

Status: done

## Outcome
`evals/results/brainstorm/base-<case>-<model>` hold ten runs per cell — clean, or after one `-lan1` reported as they are — four cases × sonnet and opus — against the unchanged `brainstorm` skill and `brainstormer` agent, on the instrument, sources and `evals/run.sh` bytes task 01 piloted, on `claude` 2.1.286 and within the $100 cap, with every run's whole answer, agent report and models saved, so a later repair can be compared against them. **No conclusion is drawn here.**

## Scope
- In: the seven prerequisite cells and the Command's cell, their per-cell checks, saved answers under `evals/results/brainstorm/_answers/`, `_reports/` and `_models/`, archives and logs under `_kept/`, and the deletion of this task's kept directories after the review.
- Out: any change to `evals/brainstorm/`, the sources or `evals/run.sh`; the pilots; conclusions; the repair.

## Coverage
- CP-02

## Ownership
- Create: `evals/results/brainstorm/base-<case>-<model>` (and any `-lan1`, `-quota`, `-reboot`), the driver and its log in `evals/results/brainstorm/_kept/t02/`, and the `base-*` files in the shared `evals/results/brainstorm/_answers/`, `_reports/`, `_models/` and `_kept/` that task 01 created
- Read: task 01's Receipt (digests, ceilings, the probe and `AskUserQuestion` results); `specs/research-eval-baseline/task-02-measure-baseline.md` Step 3 and `specs/research-repair/task-04-measure-after.md` Step 3 (re-run rules)

## Steps
1. **Guards before every paid run**: task 01 is `done`; the `evals/brainstorm` digest, the sources digest (`skills/brainstorm`, `skills/specs`, `agents/brainstormer.md` under `packages/spec/src/claude`) and `evals/run.sh`'s `sha256` equal task 01's `evals-brainstorm-digest:`, `sources-digest:` and `run-sh-sha256:` lines; `node --version` is `v22.23.3` at `/opt/homebrew/opt/node@22/bin/node` (the Command puts it first on `PATH`); `command -v git` is `/opt/homebrew/bin/git`; the session has `DISABLE_AUTOUPDATER=1`; `claude --version` is `2.1.286`. Only before the first cell: no `evals/results/brainstorm/base-*` directory exists; record `node evals/brainstorm/budget.mjs spent`.
2. Run the seven prerequisite cells — every cell except `base-mot-duong-agent-opus` — **one at a time** in the order of the Command's `C` list × `sonnet`, `opus`, skipping a cell already finished (resume) — clean, read, saved and archived, or, when its `-lan1` exists, its archived re-run ended whatever that run's state (plan D-05: nothing runs a third time): after the guards and `node evals/brainstorm/budget.mjs check <its ceiling + the Command cell's ceiling>` exits 0, run `DISABLE_AUTOUPDATER=1 evals/run.sh brainstorm --with-skill specs --with-agent brainstormer --out base-<case>-<model> --model <model> --judge-model sonnet --runs 10 --threshold 0 --ablation none --max-cost-usd <its ceiling> --allow-tools Bash Edit Write AskUserQuestion WebSearch WebFetch --case <case> --keep-temp`, the ceiling being `node evals/brainstorm/ceiling.mjs <model> <skill|agent>`; then `claude --version` still `2.1.286`; then, free and in this order: `partial: false` and ten clean runs; `node evals/brainstorm/read-traces.mjs <dir>` exits 0; `node evals/brainstorm/save-answers.mjs --answers evals/results/brainstorm/_answers --reports evals/results/brainstorm/_reports --models evals/results/brainstorm/_models <dir>` exits 0; `tar -czf evals/results/brainstorm/_kept/<cell>.tar.gz -C /private/tmp $(node evals/brainstorm/read-traces.mjs --kept <dir>)` — otherwise Step 3. The driver and its log live under `evals/results/brainstorm/_kept/t02/`, made with `mkdir -p` before the first cell (plan D-06). Then run the Command via `bash -c` on this file's own text.
3. **Re-run rule** (plan D-05): an unclean run, an error exit or an incomplete `init` (the skill, the agent or a tool of the case's `allowed_tools` other than `AskUserQuestion` missing) → the directory saved and archived under `-lan1` and the cell run once more; a second such init → stop; a usage limit → `-quota`, stop; a machine restart mid-cell → `-reboot` (cost counted, runs in no count) and the cell run again; a cell stopped `partial` without an error (its ceiling) → reported, not re-run; nothing runs a third time. When the Command fails after its paid cell for another reason and no `-lan1` of it exists, rename it `-lan1`, save and archive it, and run the Command again; if that fails too, stop for the user.
4. After a fresh review returns PASS: delete the kept directory of every run of every `base-*` cell (and `-lan1`) by exact `dirname(dirname(tracePath))` from its `result.json`, `chmod -R u+rwX` then `rm -rf`, never a glob; the archives stay until GATE-DONE.
5. Report from the Command's output, without a conclusion: per cell every grader's count over clean runs with the primary set marked, on the skill path the runs with `skill-loaded=none` listed apart with their count and never removed from the counts (plan D-01; `summarize.mjs` reads `co-goi-skill` as "Skill invoked", which on the two history cases is a repeat load, so the skill-in-context count comes from `skill-loaded=`), `can-plan-sang-specs` reported as the description-to-body gap case apart from compliance counts, `askuser=` calls with their question counts and results, `askuser-errored=` beside `khong-askuser` and `khong-hoi-lai`, `chat-questions=`, Skill and Agent calls, the agent path's `report-src=` totals, mutations, answer lengths, cost with and without the judge, `claudeVersion` and the `summarize.mjs` lines; the reader's `decisive` line of every run failing a primary grader; the total spent.
6. Write the Receipt: Command, `Exit: 0`, Base and Head from `node .claude/scripts/provenance.cjs`, the Command's output, each prerequisite cell's budget line, exit, `read-traces.mjs` and saver lines, and one `Artifact:` line with a `sha256:` line directly beneath for every `base-*/result.json` (any `-lan1`, `-quota`, `-reboot` included) and every answers, reports and models file (an answers or reports file's `headers: <n>` after its `sha256:`); set `Status: done`; run the Stop gate (`printf '{"hook_event_name":"Stop","cwd":"%s","session_id":"manual-check","featureName":"brainstorm-eval-baseline"}' "$PWD" | CLAUDE_PROJECT_DIR="$PWD" node .claude/hooks/spec-gate.cjs`) and require empty stdout.

## Acceptance
- AC-02: the Command exits 0; `read-traces.mjs --integrity` prints eight lines with `instrument=current`, `named=true` and either `partial=false runs=10 errored=0` or `retried=true`, and `claude-versions=1`; every failing primary grader's `decisive` line and every run's saved answer are in the record; the total spent is within $100.
- Every re-run or set-aside cell is named; unclean runs are in no count. **No conclusion is drawn here.**

## Dependencies
- task-01-four-brainstorm-cases-pilot.md

- Prerequisite runs: the seven cells of Step 2 and any `-lan1`, `-quota` or `-reboot`, each recorded in the Receipt with exit, `result.json` path, `sha256`, the budget line before it and its free `read-traces.mjs` and saver lines
- Named probe: the task-01, digest, sources, `run-sh-sha256`, `node`, `git`, session-autoupdater and `claude` guards; `read-traces.mjs` and `--integrity` over the seven prerequisite cells before payment (their traces and their completeness read before any money is spent); `ceiling.mjs` and `budget.mjs check`; the saver and the archive of the paid cell; `read-traces.mjs`, `--integrity` and `summarize.mjs` over the eight cells; `budget.mjs spent`
- Reachability: known — task 01's pilots ran the same harness, cases, flags and sources and its probe showed a replayed history carries the skill text; the reader proves from each run's `init` that the skill or the agent and the granted tools were registered
- Oracle: every guard passes; both pre-payment readings exit 0; `check` prints a total within 100; the invocation, the saver, the archive, both readings, `summarize.mjs` and `spent` exit 0; eight integrity lines `instrument=current` and `claude-versions=1`
- Counterexample: a changed instrument, source or `evals/run.sh`, another `node`, `git` or `claude`, a session without `DISABLE_AUTOUPDATER=1`, a prerequisite cell missing, with lost traces, short, errored without its `-lan1` or stopped `partial` by its ceiling, or spending past $100 fail before the paid cell; a stored grader differing from `evals/brainstorm`, an unclean cell without its `-lan1`, or two `claude` versions make `--integrity` exit 1; a run whose `init` lacks the skill, the agent or a granted tool makes the reader exit 1
- Artifacts: every `base-*/result.json` and every answers, reports and models file, gitignored, each on its own `Artifact:` line with a `sha256:` line directly beneath (an answers or reports file's `headers: <n>` after it); kept directories deleted in Step 4, archives kept until GATE-DONE

## Failure Protocol
On a failed Step or Verification Plan run: stop; do not widen scope, change the Command, or weaken a test; record observed versus expected; repair only the cited cause; after three failed rounds, stop and ask the user. A cell that fails under Step 3 follows that rule, not a repair round. A guard showing a changed instrument, source, `evals/run.sh`, `node` or `claude`, a usage limit, a cell stopped by its ceiling, or traces lost without an archive stops the task for the user. Never move or delete a pilot, a baseline result or any file an `Artifact:` line names.

Step 3: no cell needed a re-run; no `base-*-lan1`, `-quota` or `-reboot` directory exists. Step 4, after the review below: the 80 kept directories of the eight cells were deleted by exact `dirname(dirname(tracePath))` from each `result.json` (`chmod -R u+rwX`, then `rm -rf` on each path; each checked first to be a direct child of `/private/tmp` and present in its cell's archive); the archives `_kept/base-*.tar.gz` are now the only copies of the baseline traces, so a re-reading first needs `tar -xzf evals/results/brainstorm/_kept/<cell>.tar.gz -C /private/tmp`. The probes' and pilots' kept directories stay until GATE-DONE.

Step 5, without a conclusion (the fenced block holds every count, `decisive` line and cost): primary counts by cell — `duyet-khong-trien-khai` sonnet and opus 10/10 on all five; `can-plan-sang-specs` (the gap case) sonnet `co-goi-skill` 0, `bao-goi-specs` 0, `khong-goi-specs-ngam` 10, `khong-phuong-an` 10, `ngan-gon` 0, and opus 1, 2, 10, 8, 0; `ne-cau-hoi` sonnet and opus `khong-hoi-ky-thuat` 10, `co-khuyen-nghi` 10, `dua-muc-tieu` 10, `ghi-gia-dinh` 0 and 2; `mot-duong-agent` sonnet `co-goi-agent` 10, `mot-duong` 0, `khong-bu-nhin` 9, `nhan-kha-thi` 0, `khong-tu-duyet` 10, and opus 5, 2, 8, 3, 10. No run called `AskUserQuestion` (not offered by the eval host); `askuser-errored=0` in every cell; no run changed the workspace (`mutations=none`). On the history cases the skill came from the replayed history in 40 of 40 runs; on the one-turn gap case only one opus run called the skill. Opus on the agent path called `brainstormer` in 5 of 10 runs and the skill in 6; every `brainstormer` call carried no route (`no-route`). The parent models were `claude-sonnet-5-5` and `claude-opus-5-5`, and `brainstormer` ran on its parent's model (`_models/`). Hand-read misreads of the frozen instrument are listed in the plan's Known limits (`mot-duong` and `ghi-gia-dinh` counts are lower bounds; `khong-bu-nhin` errs both ways; one `bao-goi-specs` false fail). Total spent: $18.2245 of $100, task 02 $11.7094.

A fresh `code-auditor` review returned FAIL on one Medium finding (the plan's hand-read misread entry gave wrong hand totals and missed misreads); after the entry was rewritten without totals and with the missing runs, it re-reviewed and returned PASS: AC-02 holds against the fenced output, the guards and pre-payment order hold, every cell has ten clean runs, its saver files and an archive whose 80 traces match the kept directories byte for byte, scope stayed in `evals/results/brainstorm/**`, and Step 4's deletion was safe.
